> 代码位置：F:\workspace\ai\deer-flow
> 功能描述：DeerFlow 调用链深度分析。
> 重点关注：智能体全生命周期、M1–M9 九模块，以及调用链、状态流转、并发隔离、资源生命周期与失败边界。

# 1. DeerFlow 智能体实现深度分析：阅读指南与边界

DeerFlow 将模型驱动的多轮工具执行组织成可保存状态、委派任务、请求人工输入并持续输出事件的 agent harness。主要使用者是服务操作者、通过 Web/IM 提交任务的用户及直接嵌入 Harness 的 Python 调用方。生成工具调用只是执行意图；授权、分发、回填、持久终态确认和资源清理是不同阶段，不能合并成“模型已完成任务”。

分析固定于提交 **5f6d7c06e0896f4c92aebe7bfb01f5a5d21952ae**。本次仅静态阅读源码、配置、依赖锁和已有测试，没有执行项目模块、服务、测试、容器、迁移或外部业务能力。测试引用代表预期契约，不代表本次通过。实际部署配置、模型、实例数和线上表现未知，不展示真实凭据或隐私数据。

D1 是触发点到执行规则和副作用的调用链；D2 是状态读写与 reducer；D3 是单 Agent、子 Agent、多 Gateway 的并发隔离；D4 是资源建立、持有及释放；D5 是失败和易误用边界。九模块按五维展开，全生命周期另外覆盖进程、thread、run、模型/工具、子智能体和后台任务六层。

代码事实、条件推断与未知分别标明。注释或文档与实现冲突时明确指出。源码链接显示仓库相对路径及本次快照的行号范围，跳转路径相对本文所在 docs 目录。分析不把局部覆盖宣称为全仓库完整覆盖：核心文件逐函数展开，关联文件按调用链展开；各 IM 协议、全部扩展业务及整个前端不在逐函数范围内。

Checkpoint 是图状态，不是外部副作用事务日志；run lease 决定运行写入权，sandbox ownership lease 决定容器销毁权；SSE 结束、业务完成、记忆更新完成和容器销毁各有独立条件。“恢复”既可能继续中断图，也可能仅将失联 run 收敛为 error，必须区分。

# 2. 总体架构与请求入口

## 2.1 工程形态与依赖版本

后端使用 Python，Harness 独立分发为 deerflow-harness，App 是 FastAPI Gateway 和 IM 集成；前端为 Next.js。App 可以导入 deerflow，Harness 不反向导入 app，仓库边界测试检查这一约束。LangChain 提供 create_agent 和中间件，LangGraph 提供图、channel、checkpoint、中断。声明要求 Python >=3.12，源码自身版本为 2.3.0-dev，不能据此确认线上版本。[backend/pyproject.toml:1–45](../backend/pyproject.toml#L1-L45) [backend/packages/harness/pyproject.toml:1–75](../backend/packages/harness/pyproject.toml#L1-L75) [frontend/package.json:1–45](../frontend/package.json#L1-L45) [backend/tests/test_harness_boundary.py:1–47](../backend/tests/test_harness_boundary.py#L1-L47)

锁定版本为 langchain 1.3.14、langgraph 1.2.14、langgraph-checkpoint 4.3.0、FastAPI 0.136.1、sse-starlette 3.3.4。声明范围和锁定结果不同，例如 LangGraph 的声明是 >=1.2.9,<1.3；本机实际依赖环境未执行确认。[backend/uv.lock:996–1004](../backend/uv.lock#L996-L1004) [backend/uv.lock:1004–1012](../backend/uv.lock#L1004-L1012) [backend/uv.lock:1006–1014](../backend/uv.lock#L1006-L1014) [backend/uv.lock:858–866](../backend/uv.lock#L858-L866) [backend/uv.lock:867–875](../backend/uv.lock#L867-L875)

## 2.2 请求入口与执行环境

下图区分 HTTP 边界和进程内执行。

~~~text
浏览器 / HTTP SDK → nginx :2026
                    ├─ /                  → Frontend :3000
                    ├─ /api/langgraph/*    → 重写 /api/* → Gateway :8001
                    └─ 其他 /api/*         → Gateway :8001
                                                → 认证/归属/准入
                                                → 内嵌 LangGraph runtime
                                                → 模型、工具、沙箱、checkpoint
Python 调用方 → DeerFlowClient → 同进程图执行（无 HTTP/SSE）
可选 Studio → langgraph.json → deerflow.agents:make_lead_agent
~~~

实际观察：常规服务的 runtime 嵌入 Gateway；预期背景容易被理解成独立 LangGraph Server。nginx 明确将 /api/langgraph/* 重写后交给 Gateway；langgraph.json 保留另一入口，声明 lead_agent、认证、HTTP App 和 checkpointer 工厂。模型和容器可以跨进程，普通 tool 也可以只做进程内计算；工具名称本身不提供隔离保证。Provisioner 仅在对应 backend 选择下参与。[docker/nginx/nginx.conf:92–125](../docker/nginx/nginx.conf#L92-L125) [docker/nginx/nginx.conf:346–365](../docker/nginx/nginx.conf#L346-L365) [backend/langgraph.json:1–21](../backend/langgraph.json#L1-L21) [backend/app/gateway/deps.py:772–1127](../backend/app/gateway/deps.py#L772-L1127) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:349–384](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L349-L384)

## 2.3 输入、身份与 worker

stream_run/create_run → start_run → normalize_input/build_run_config → RunManager 准入 → 注册 run_agent。返回 RunRecord 表示接受运行，实际模型执行在 worker 中发生。服务端先剥离客户伪造的内部上下文和状态元数据，再注入认证身份；持久历史不能授予当前权限。调度任务和 MCP 通知也经宿主入口建立受控 context，而非让模型自行构造可信能力。[backend/app/gateway/routers/thread_runs.py:1091–1138](../backend/app/gateway/routers/thread_runs.py#L1091-L1138) [backend/app/gateway/routers/thread_runs.py:1073–1088](../backend/app/gateway/routers/thread_runs.py#L1073-L1088) [backend/app/gateway/services.py:2295–3064](../backend/app/gateway/services.py#L2295-L3064) [backend/app/gateway/services.py:769–848](../backend/app/gateway/services.py#L769-L848) [backend/app/gateway/services.py:1479–1608](../backend/app/gateway/services.py#L1479-L1608) [backend/app/gateway/services.py:1261–1340](../backend/app/gateway/services.py#L1261-L1340) [backend/app/gateway/services.py:3065–3168](../backend/app/gateway/services.py#L3065-L3168)

# 3. 全生命周期：创建、运行、等待、恢复与销毁

## 3.1 进程创建与启动失败

app.lifespan 持有宿主服务；deps.langgraph_runtime 以 AsyncExitStack 持有运行依赖。顺序为冻结 checkpoint 模式/快照频率 → bridge → engine → checkpointer → Store → repositories/runtime → RunManager → 孤儿 run 收敛 → heartbeat。engine 先建立让创建数据库先于 checkpointer 建表；回调先登记再初始化，覆盖部分启动失败，释放则逆序执行。[backend/app/gateway/app.py:625–635](../backend/app/gateway/app.py#L625-L635) [backend/app/gateway/deps.py:772–1127](../backend/app/gateway/deps.py#L772-L1127)

必须先 drain 活动 run 再 close checkpoint pool，否则仍在写 checkpoint 的图任务会访问已关闭连接。宿主还分别关闭记忆、批任务、通道、MCP 和沙箱。有的清理受时间预算约束，有的已开始操作必须排空后再抛出取消，不能将 close 一概理解为立即完成。[backend/app/gateway/deps.py:484–522](../backend/app/gateway/deps.py#L484-L522) [backend/app/gateway/app.py:293–325](../backend/app/gateway/app.py#L293-L325) [backend/app/gateway/app.py:587–623](../backend/app/gateway/app.py#L587-L623)

## 3.2 对象和状态边界

Thread 是持久会话，run 是一次执行。create_thread 建立元数据和用户归属，checkpoint 保存图状态；同线程多轮有不同 run_id 和 owner_worker_id。tool_call_id 只保证响应内关联，provider 可以重复使用，因此委派还需 dispatch_message_id。文件和 sandbox 身份按 user/thread 组织。[backend/app/gateway/routers/threads.py:1008–1019](../backend/app/gateway/routers/threads.py#L1008-L1019) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:276–284](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L276-L284) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:226–234](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L226-L234) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/identity.py:26–40](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/identity.py#L26-L40)

下图表示状态分支，不是每次请求必经的直线。

~~~text
输入 → 准入 ├─ 同幂等键匹配 → 返回已有 run
            ├─ 冲突/越权/非法 → 拒绝
            └─ pending → running
                          ├─ 正常图结束 → success → 等待终态提交/清理
                          ├─ 人工中断 → waiting_for_input → 合法恢复/拒绝
                          ├─ 超时 → timeout
                          ├─ 异常 → error
                          └─ interrupt/rollback → interrupted 或失败收敛
失联 owner / 过期 lease → reconcile → error（不自动重跑工具）
~~~

RunStatus 没有单独的 cancelled 枚举，取消按具体控制路径映射；waiting_for_input 也不是业务完成。finalizing 和持久终态 ACK 是状态之外的条件，内存已改变不代表屏障已释放。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/schemas.py:21–29](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/schemas.py#L21-L29) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:1420–1469](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L1420-L1469) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:709–733](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L709-L733) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:692–695](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L692-L695)

## 3.3 准入、重复请求与追加输入

_admit_thread_operation 仅接受 reject/interrupt/rollback。它建立 pending 记录，在 thread/run 局部锁与持久存储原子操作下决定是否接收。重复 idempotency_key 必须属于相同 user/thread；支持相应请求快照和 execution fence 才能承担对应幂等契约，旧第三方 RunStore 缺少能力时会拒绝。后续请求还需等待旧 run finalization，不能只看 running 标志。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:2413–2704](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L2413-L2704) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:2306–2358](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L2306-L2358) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/store/base.py:521–573](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/store/base.py#L521-L573) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:1862–1893](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L1862-L1893)

Queue/Steer 是 run 内输入控制，不是 multitask_strategy=enqueue。create_run_input → submit_run_input → RunInputs/RunInputControl → RunInputMiddleware 在安全执行边界消费，并记录 applied_run_inputs。取消输入、重试输入有独立控制面；这不证明外部工具可被任意即时替换。RunInputMiddleware 在 after_agent 反序阶段优先决定是否继续，随后才进入其他清理。[backend/app/gateway/routers/thread_runs.py:110–113](../backend/app/gateway/routers/thread_runs.py#L110-L113) [backend/app/gateway/run_inputs.py:39–65](../backend/app/gateway/run_inputs.py#L39-L65) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/input_control.py:81–89](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/input_control.py#L81-L89) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/run_input_middleware.py:80–88](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/run_input_middleware.py#L80-L88) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:485–822](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L485-L822)

## 3.4 人工等待与恢复

Clarification 工具 wrapper 处理 ask_clarification；非交互运行不能等待无人响应的确认。工具审批另有 owner、run_id、request_id、interrupt-ID。answer_approval 检查归属及活动状态；validate_resume_request 对照 checkpoint 的待处理中断，不能仅因客户提交 resume 就执行。批准是允许继续动作，拒绝、过期和无效回复不是同一种结果。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/clarification_middleware.py:557–580](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/clarification_middleware.py#L557-L580) [backend/app/gateway/tool_approvals.py:55–101](../backend/app/gateway/tool_approvals.py#L55-L101) [backend/app/gateway/hitl_approval.py:50–64](../backend/app/gateway/hitl_approval.py#L50-L64) [backend/app/gateway/hitl_approval.py:249–335](../backend/app/gateway/hitl_approval.py#L249-L335)

恢复重新装配图和服务端 context，再解释 checkpoint 的 pending writes/interrupt。全部拒绝的旧审批在 HITL 已关闭时有受服务端控制的 escape，只用于消费拒绝而非授权旧动作。具体从哪个节点续跑取决于 checkpoint；保存过历史不能证明外部动作恰好执行一次。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:3226–3272](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L3226-L3272) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:3291–3403](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L3291-L3403) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:1005–2499](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L1005-L2499)

## 3.5 取消、回滚、断连和崩溃

cancel_run 的 interrupt 停止并保留 checkpoint，rollback 还恢复运行前图状态。RunManager 区分本地和远端 owner，建立持久取消条件后通知 worker；_finish_cancellation 尝试取消该执行的沙箱进程，再处理回滚和终态。收到取消请求不表示底层线程、子任务、远端动作或回滚已经完成。[backend/app/gateway/routers/thread_runs.py:1367–1425](../backend/app/gateway/routers/thread_runs.py#L1367-L1425) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:2089–2290](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L2089-L2290) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:1124–1184](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L1124-L1184) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:3404–3515](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L3404-L3515)

图状态回滚不撤销工具已发出的任意网络动作。取消 await 不能自动终止已经运行的线程；代码使用 drain 和 execution lease 约束收尾，仍不能推导出全局补偿事务。SSE 断连仅在 creator、on_disconnect=cancel、仍活动等条件下取消，观察者 join 和 replay gap 不应终止原生产者。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/utils/file_io.py:82–110](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/utils/file_io.py#L82-L110) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/lease.py:819–829](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/lease.py#L819-L829) [backend/app/gateway/services.py:3261–3383](../backend/app/gateway/services.py#L3261-L3383)

run lease 丢失后旧 worker 被 fence，不能写自己的终态覆盖已接管状态。启动/周期 reconcile 将符合条件的 orphan run 标 error 并恢复终态可见性，不自动重放原 run。容器 ownership 是另一套销毁权机制。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:3146–3179](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L3146-L3179) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:2798–2909](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L2798-L2909) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:3495–3523](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L3495-L3523)

## 3.6 结束后的后台与资源生命周期

worker finalization 确认完成记录和持久终态授权才发布 END；嵌套 finally 关闭审批/journal，结束 stream producer lease，释放 sandbox execution lease，断开 graph/runtime 强引用，并安排 bridge 60 秒延后清理及默认五分钟 run registry 清理。业务返回、持久完成和内存回收有不同时间。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:2021–2481](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L2021-L2481) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:236–287](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L236-L287) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:3003–3039](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L3003-L3039)

Memory after_agent 提交后台工作，返回不保证 facts 已落盘；sandbox release 可只是进 Warm Pool。子任务 completed/failed 与父任务验收也不同。优雅关闭依赖各组件收尾策略；硬退出没有 Python finally 必然执行的保证。

## 3.7 分支与销毁

_branch_thread_with_reservation 在 thread-operation reservation 下复制分支状态与元数据。fork-restored sandbox 值由 SandboxMiddleware 识别并跳过父容器释放，避免子线程归还父资源。[backend/app/gateway/routers/threads.py:1133–1351](../backend/app/gateway/routers/threads.py#L1133-L1351) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py:462–493](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py#L462-L493)

删除在 delete reservation 内统一 owner，先清审批并抑制批任务续跑，再删合法 ID 对应文件、checkpoint，以及历史和元数据。checkpoint 删除失败返回 500 并保留 owner 信息；run/events/feedback/thread_meta 存在 best-effort 分支，所以不是跨存储原子清空。非法 legacy thread ID 跳过本地路径拼接，继续安全的存储清理。[backend/app/gateway/routers/threads.py:787–809](../backend/app/gateway/routers/threads.py#L787-L809) [backend/app/gateway/routers/threads.py:828–975](../backend/app/gateway/routers/threads.py#L828-L975)

## 3.8 目标续跑与结束判定

一次 run 可以包含用户可见回合和后续隐藏目标回合。run_agent 首先 _stream_once，再检查 input_control 与 pending pause；只有没有暂停、没有 abort、没有模型错误回退时，才调用 _prepare_goal_continuation_input。该函数要求存在 checkpointer 和 active goal，且 scheduled expected_goal 仍是同一实例；没有 durable assistant 回合结束凭据、最后仍在向用户提问或递归预算失败时，不继续消耗目标回合。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:1812–1867](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L1812-L1867) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:2832–3104](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L2832-L3104)

目标判断不是直接信任模型一句“完成”。实现读取 checkpoint/messages，记录可见对话与 AI 证据签名，调用 evaluator；格式错误最多重试一次，重试前先确认目标和对话没变。判断后再次比较 goal instance、checkpoint ID 与 conversation signature，用户新消息或清目标的操作应使旧判断停下。satisfied 返回 _GoalCompletionCandidate，尚不是无条件清空 goal；未满足时根据 blocker、max_continuations、no_progress_count 和 hard stop 决定是否追加 continuation message。消息先脱敏再记次数，脱敏失败不发原始目标、不花该次 continuation 预算。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:2961–3102](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L2961-L3102) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:2746–2759](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L2746-L2759)

_persist_goal_evaluation 在 goal_thread_lock 内重读当前目标，以新鲜 continuation_count 计算下一次数，并传 expected_checkpoint_id 给 write_thread_goal；后者写新 ID、父 checkpoint、channel version 和 metadata。预期 ID 检查是写前冲突检测，不能据此称数据库级原子 CAS；goal_thread_lock 的契约也仅是当前 event loop，跨实例还依赖 Gateway 的运行准入/写入 reservation。持久次数后又读消息签名，若竞态出现，仅记录 stand-down，避免把同一次尝试再加一次。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:2760–2819](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L2760-L2819) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/goal.py:88–92](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/goal.py#L88-L92) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/goal.py:851–925](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/goal.py#L851-L925)

## 3.9 预算、工具错误与产物交付

graph 正常返回和 run.success 之间还有宿主判断。ToolErrorHandlingMiddleware 将普通 Exception 转成关联 ToolMessage，但 GraphBubbleUp 原样传播，保证 interrupt 不被“工具报错”吃掉；asyncio 取消也不能被当普通业务错误强行吞掉。模型错误回退消息由 worker 识别并使 run.error，不能因为有一条可显示的 AI 文本就宣告成功。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py:340–357](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py#L340-L357) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py:359–377](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py#L359-L377) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:1871–1886](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L1871-L1886)

loop/token/safety/subagent/model-length 的 hard guard 会移除调用并写 stop_reason；图可能正常结束，worker 将 cap 原因带进 RunRecord，且隐藏目标回合不会清掉用户回合已发生的 cap。主图 GraphRecursionError 是 error + recursion_capped，会列出已创建但未呈现的产物并暂停目标；子图另有“可用部分文本转 completed+turn_capped”的分支，因此不能把父子状态枚举直接等价。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:1888–1907](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L1888-L1907) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:1936–1996](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L1936-L1996) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:1758–2246](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L1758-L2246)

正常结束前，worker 比较运行前 workspace snapshot 和产出路径，再将交付工具中呈现的路径与产物匹配。_delivery_content_with_outputs 以至少一个产出被匹配决定 satisfied；若有产出却未呈现，_delivery_error 返回错误并令 run.error。它不检查每个产物内容的业务正确性；初始 snapshot 截断且没有可确认产出时会标 verification_skipped，而非假定已全面核验。finalization 还需把 delivery receipt/完成证据持久化，这些是源码现有行为，本次没有执行产物交付。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:392–421](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L392-L421) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:422–428](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L422-L428) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:310–358](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L310-L358)


# 4. 模块深度拆解

## 4.1. 九个核心模块

### M1. Lead Agent 装配链

#### D1 调用链追踪

发布入口是 agents.__init__.make_lead_agent(config)，惰性导入 lead_agent.agent.make_lead_agent；后者返回 assemble_lead_agent(config).graph。assemble 选择 AppConfig，冻结进程 checkpoint 模式和 cadence，注入内部模式键，再调用 _assemble_lead_agent。_make_lead_agent 是绕过该冻结步骤的内部 graph-only 包装，也调用 _assemble_lead_agent；**它不是发布入口必经的一层**。create_deerflow_agent 是独立的纯参数 SDK 工厂，直接调用同一 LangChain primitive，而不是被 _make_lead_agent 调用。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/__init__.py:20–33](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/__init__.py#L20-L33) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:868–872](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L868-L872) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:873–913](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L873-L913) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:914–923](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L914-L923) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/factory.py:67–204](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/factory.py#L67-L204)

实际装配过程为 _get_runtime_config 合并 configurable/context → resolve_config_user_id → 加载 custom Agent/skill 权限 → _resolve_model_name/_authorize_model_name → create_chat_model → get_available_tools → 交互策略过滤/附加 memory 与项目工具 → apply_tool_authorization → assemble_deferred_tools → build_middlewares → narrow_declared_tools/HITL → apply_prompt_template → normalize_middleware_state_schemas → verify_declared_tool_view → create_agent(model, tools, middleware, system_prompt, state_schema, context_schema=dict) → _complete_assembly。工具配置通过反射解析实际实现，MCP 取已初始化目录，host grant 控制调度工具；模型解析和 schema 选择不是简单字符串透传。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:990–1483](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L990-L1483) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/tools/tools.py:163–380](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/tools/tools.py#L163-L380) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/tools/tools.py:116–145](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/tools/tools.py#L116-L145)

~~~text
装配
 ├─ 无模型配置 → ValueError，不能生成图
 ├─ 请求模型未知 → 回退首个模型 → 继续授权判断
 ├─ 模型被拒 → 搜索可用且 model:use 允许的替代项
 │               └─ 无替代项 → 按 fail_closed 抛错或保留原选择
 ├─ is_bootstrap → 窄技能集合 + setup_agent；不拥有普通 thread skill 投影
 └─ 常规 Agent
     ├─ 命名 Agent 且非 GitHub webhook → 可附加 update_agent
     ├─ allowed_subagents=[] → 即使请求开启也禁用 task
     └─ 完成工具/中间件/Prompt 装配 → create_agent
~~~

图形态取决于实际 middleware、schema 和工具集，而非只有 model：is_plan_mode 加 Todo；subagent_enabled 加 task 与限流；memory_enabled 决定读写链；vision、summarization、token budget、PII、审批、deferred MCP、run input control 和扩展均可改变节点或 wrapper。model 还影响 vision/推理能力和绑定参数。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:485–822](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L485-L822)

#### D2 状态流转

装配本身不执行 ThreadState 更新；它决定 state_schema、channel 和后续读写者。cfg 是 configurable 的副本，再由 context 覆盖；thinking/reasoning 采用“键是否存在”区分显式 false 与缺省，而模型名采用请求 → Agent 配置 → 全局首项。装配会回写有效 subagent_enabled、metadata 和 tracing callbacks 到调用配置，故输入 config 不是完全不可变。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:219–227](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L219-L227) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:167–182](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L167-L182) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:990–1483](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L990-L1483)

实际观察：LeadAgentAssembly 是 graph、descriptor、effective_model；预期包含 resolved_extensions。后者只是装配时解析的局部对象，不是 dataclass 字段。无 observer 且无 capture 请求时 descriptor=None，不能假定每次装配都有完整描述。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:96–104](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L96-L104) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:924–989](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L924-L989)

#### D3 并发与隔离

工厂不是运行准入锁，Gateway 用 RunManager 防止同线程冲突；独立 SDK 调用者须自行建立相应边界。模式和快照频率进程冻结，客户配置不能任意切换共享存储解释方式。工具可能来自进程级单例/缓存，_ensure_sync_invocable_tool 用 threading.Lock 双检只安装一次同步包装，描述修改则克隆工具，避免一个 Agent 的提示污染其他图。subagent_runtime 显式传入时共享容量边界，省略时保留应用兼容的进程级后备。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:2413–2704](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L2413-L2704) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/tools/tools.py:72–87](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/tools/tools.py#L72-L87) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/tools/tools.py:107–115](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/tools/tools.py#L107-L115) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/factory.py:67–204](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/factory.py#L67-L204)

#### D4 生命周期

每次装配产生图、中间件对象及可能的 descriptor/model client 引用，不在此启动整个 Gateway 的资源系统。worker 保存图到本次执行，finalization 主动断开 runtime/config/journal 强引用，并安排回收；调用方持有的 SDK graph 则由调用方管理。create_deerflow_agent 不加载 YAML，也不安装全局依赖；带 durable batch 的显式 SubagentRuntime 必须先 start 或进入 async context。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:236–287](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L236-L287) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/factory.py:67–204](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/factory.py#L67-L204)

#### D5 异常与边界

独立工厂的 middleware= 是完整接管，不能与 features/extra_middleware 混用，也会绕过自动链的策略安装。该入口拒绝“delta + 非空 checkpointer”，因为没有应用侧模式标记和 fail-closed 兼容门禁；无 saver 的 ephemeral delta 才允许。另一个误读点是 _resolve_model_name 注释称无模型返回 None，函数实际抛 ValueError；应以函数体为准。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/factory.py:67–204](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/factory.py#L67-L204) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:228–242](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L228-L242)

### M2. 中间件钩子契约与洋葱模型

#### D1 调用链追踪

Lead build_middlewares 首先调用 build_lead_runtime_middlewares → _build_runtime_middlewares 形成共享基础，再追加 Lead 专属项，最后 compose_with_extensions 合并扩展并检查 ordering。子 Agent 的 _create_agent 则调用 build_subagent_runtime_middlewares。不要用旧流程文档中固定编号解释当前链。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:485–822](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L485-L822) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py:378–573](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py#L378-L573) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py:603–893](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py#L603-L893) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:1131–1326](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L1131-L1326) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/extensions/stack.py:134–171](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/extensions/stack.py#L134-L171)

| 钩子 | 触发位置与当前用途 |
| --- | --- |
| before_agent | 每次图执行开始的前置节点；线程数据、sandbox retain、技能激活等 |
| before_model | 每轮模型前；摘要压缩、预热启动、上下文和工具状态准备 |
| wrap_model_call | 包住一次模型调用；请求投影、告警注入、内容兼容、模型错误重试 |
| after_model | 响应写入后、决定工具路由前；截断委派、循环/预算/安全终止判断 |
| after_agent | 本次图执行结束的后置节点；续输入决定、记忆入队和资源归还 |
| wrap_tool_call | 实际工具 handler 周围；授权、审批、审计、进度、错误转换和沙箱更新 |

正常一轮执行顺序如下，跳转、中断和短路会改变实际经过的节点。

~~~text
START
 → before_agent：0 → N
 → [每轮 before_model：0 → N]
 → wrap_model_call 进入：外层0 → 内层N → MODEL
 ← wrap_model_call 返回：内层N → 外层0
 → after_model：N → 0
    ├─ 有允许的 tool_calls
    │   → 每个工具 wrap_tool_call：外 → 内 → handler → 内 → 外
    │   → ToolMessage/Command → reducer 提交 → 下一轮 before_model
    ├─ 人工中断 → checkpoint/等待 → 后续恢复
    └─ 无继续动作 → after_agent：N → 0 → END
~~~

共享基础顺序为 InputSanitization → KnowledgeScope → ToolOutputBudget → ToolResultSanitization → 可选 PII → ThreadData → Lead 的 Uploads → Sandbox → DanglingToolCall → LLMErrorHandling → 可选 receipt/artifact/authorization/guardrail → SandboxAudit → 可选 ReadBeforeWrite/HarnessGuard/ToolProgress → ToolErrorHandling → 可选 ArtifactCapture。Lead 再加 DynamicContext、SkillActivation、DurableContext、可选摘要/Todo/token usage、Title、条件 Memory/Vision/MCP、SystemMessageCoalescing、条件委派/loop/token guards、ModelContentCompatibility、定制项、TerminalResponse、ModelLength、条件 Safety、Clarification，最后可能加入 run-input 控制。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py:378–573](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py#L378-L573) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:485–822](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L485-L822)

实际观察：数量是条件装配结果，子 Agent 同样有 DanglingToolCall、SkillActivation、模型长度限制、DurableContext、日期和系统消息合并，并可启用 loop/token/摘要等；预期“主14、子4”已过时。给出可复核的静态计数例：关闭所有可选链、关闭 Agent 记忆、无 vision/deferred/扩展/输入控制时，Lead 内置列表为20，子链为16；这不是部署默认值或已运行观测。旧文档仍写14/4，作为冲突证据保留。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py:603–893](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py#L603-L893) [backend/docs/middleware-execution-flow.md:1–32](../backend/docs/middleware-execution-flow.md#L1-L32)

#### D2 状态流转

before/after 返回 dict 或 Command 的更新才经 channel/reducer 保存；wrap_model_call 的 request.override 只是当前请求视图。例如循环 warning 与系统消息合并不等同持久历史，sandbox wrapper 返回 Command 才能把 lazy ID 提交给图。ToolErrorHandling 给可见结果加结构化元数据，ToolProgress/receipt 消费这些元数据，不能从自然语言猜状态。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py:554–581](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py#L554-L581) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:940–957](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L940-L957) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py:340–357](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_error_handling_middleware.py#L340-L357)

#### D3 并发与隔离

“洋葱”描述单次调用的 wrapper 嵌套，不是全局互斥。不同工具可能并行，因此其共享字段必须有 reducer，进程共享字典必须自行同步。assert_ordering 在扩展合并后校验全部同类实例的索引，违反即 RuntimeError；扩展不能插入到校验后的缝隙中绕过约束。范围为一个最终链，而不是跨 Gateway 的分布式排序。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/extensions/ordering.py:36–59](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/extensions/ordering.py#L36-L59) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:79–99](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L79-L99)

#### D4 生命周期

before_agent/after_agent 是正常图生命周期钩子，不是任意异常必经的 try/finally。Gateway 的外层 execution lease/finalizer 负责中断、取消和异常收尾；自定义直接图调用方不能仅因装了 SandboxMiddleware 就假设拥有全部宿主清理保证。组件对象可以服务多次调用，瞬时 warning 和工具进度须按 entry/run 约束。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:2021–2481](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L2021-L2481) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/lease.py:845–865](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/lease.py#L845-L865)

#### D5 异常与边界

数据必须早于消费者、资源获取早于使用、清理晚于使用。具体可执行证据是 ToolProgress 必须在 ToolErrorHandling 外层：先由内层将异常转成带 deerflow_tool_meta 的结果，再由外层统计；颠倒会把工具失败误计成成功或丢失状态。receipt 还必须包住会短路的授权/写门禁，否则拒绝没有 receipt。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/extensions/ordering.py:61–121](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/extensions/ordering.py#L61-L121)

“Clarification 必须最后”应限定语境：它是普通业务链末端，但当前 builder 会在它后面加入 RunInputMiddleware，扩展还按锚点合并；不能继续声称它永远是最终列表最后一个对象。ThreadData 早于 Sandbox、模型前压缩早于其上下文消费者的目的仍成立，但具体受钩子类型与 wrapper 展开影响。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:485–822](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L485-L822)

### M3. ThreadState 与 Reducer 系统

#### D1 调用链追踪

create_agent 获取 get_thread_state_schema(mode) 选择 ThreadState 或动态 delta schema；工具和 middleware 产生部分更新，经字段 channel 合并，再由 checkpointer 保存。Gateway 用 CheckpointStateAccessor 读取、history 或修改；Gateway 列表走 ThreadMetaStore.search，而不是扫描所有 checkpoint channel_values。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:564–569](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L564-L569) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:990–1483](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L990-L1483) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/checkpoint_state.py:113–121](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/checkpoint_state.py#L113-L121) [backend/app/gateway/routers/threads.py:1354–1396](../backend/app/gateway/routers/threads.py#L1354-L1396)

~~~text
状态写入
 ├─ messages → AgentState 的消息合并；delta 模式改用 DeltaChannel
 ├─ 显式 Overwrite → 替换 channel 值；task_notes 仍再次规范化
 ├─ 带 reducer 字段 → 字段自己的规则
 └─ 其他字段 → 普通覆盖语义，不自动深合并嵌套 dict
读取
 ├─ 线程列表/归属/展示 → ThreadMetaStore
 └─ 图执行/状态/history → CheckpointStateAccessor → saver
~~~

#### D2 状态流转

ThreadState 继承 AgentState 的 messages 等上游字段，当前自行新增19项；下表完整列出新增字段。 [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:422–441](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L422-L441)普通字段的“覆盖”不表示整个 ThreadState 被全量替换。

| 字段 | 数据与合并语义 |
| --- | --- |
| sandbox | SandboxStateField / merge_sandbox：None 保留，相同 sandbox_id 幂等，不同 ID 抛错 |
| thread_data | workspace/uploads/outputs 宿主路径；普通可选字段 |
| title | 会话标题，普通可选字段 |
| artifacts | merge_artifacts：追加去重、保持首次顺序；空列表不会清空已有 |
| todos | merge_todos：最后非 None 值整体胜出，[] 显式清空 |
| goal | merge_goal：新 None 保留，否则替换 |
| goal_outcome | 目标结束解释，普通可选字段 |
| applied_run_inputs | run 输入应用记录，普通可选字段 |
| uploaded_files | 上传文件元信息，普通可选字段 |
| viewed_images | merge_viewed_images：按 key 更新并刷新顺序；{} 清空、None 保留 |
| promoted | merge_promoted：同 catalog/turn 合并 names；catalog 或明确 turn 改变则替换 |
| delegations | merge_delegations：按 run/call/dispatch 关联；终态不降为非终态；最多保留50项 |
| skill_context | merge_skill_context：按 path 去重刷新最近性，丢旧正文键，仅保留8个引用 |
| tool_artifacts | merge_tool_artifacts：按 handle 更新、保留首次顺序，支持 trim_to，绝对上限1000 |
| tool_artifact_processed | merge_artifacts：已处理标识追加去重 |
| task_notes | TaskNotesChannel + merge_task_notes：按 note key 合并，None 删除该 key，最多8项，值规范化为 model_report |
| task_history | 历史归档引用，普通可选字段 |
| summary_text | 压缩后的摘要文本，普通可选字段 |
| background_tasks | MCP 后台任务投影列表，普通可选字段 |

[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:422–430](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L422-L430) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:165–192](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L165-L192) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:258–298](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L258-L298) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:322–359](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L322-L359) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:383–421](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L383-L421) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/task_continuity/state.py:81–91](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/task_continuity/state.py#L81-L91) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/task_continuity/state.py:99–104](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/task_continuity/state.py#L99-L104)

todos 的历史故障有直接回归材料：流式阶段看到 write_todos 的列表，后续节点返回 todos=None，以无 reducer 的覆盖通道抹掉已有值，导致结束时消失。merge_todos 不按 task ID 合并，也不追加；完整新列表是显式替换，None 才是“不动它”。这解释了 UI 可见与最终状态不同，而非虚构客户端缓存故障。[backend/tests/test_thread_state_reducers.py:1–7](../backend/tests/test_thread_state_reducers.py#L1-L7) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:139–151](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L139-L151)

#### D3 并发与隔离

reducer 是并行图写的合并规则，不是线程锁，也不普遍可交换：todos 多个非 None 更新仍依赖写入顺序；不同 sandbox ID 被 fail-closed 拒绝，避免任意最后写者掩盖隔离错误。delegation_key 使用 (run_id,id,dispatch_message_id)，兼容旧记录时只允许受控匹配，防止重复 provider ID 低估预算。ledger 的50项上限和单 run 总预算上限配合，不能外推为无限历史准确计数。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:79–99](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L79-L99) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:235–257](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L235-L257) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/subagent_limit_middleware.py:91–103](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/subagent_limit_middleware.py#L91-L103)

#### D4 生命周期

channel_values 随 checkpoint 保存执行状态；ThreadMetaStore 保存轻量的列表、归属、状态与展示信息，适合排序过滤，不承载完整消息图。delta schema 对累积消息保存增量并按 cadence 做 snapshot，读取需物化；merge_message_writes 保持公开 add_messages 的 ID 替换、删除和 REMOVE_ALL_MESSAGES 语义。viewed_images 仅存元数据/引用，不长期复制 base64。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/persistence/thread_meta/base.py:59–67](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/persistence/thread_meta/base.py#L59-L67) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:474–530](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L474-L530) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:531–538](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L531-L538) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:62–70](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L62-L70)

#### D5 异常与边界

以下10行是冲突拒绝的实际分支，来源 [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:88–97](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L88-L97)。

~~~python
    if new is None:
        return existing
    if existing is None:
        return new

    existing_id = existing.get("sandbox_id")
    new_id = new.get("sandbox_id")
    if existing_id == new_id:
        return existing
    raise ValueError(f"Conflicting sandbox state updates: {existing_id!r} != {new_id!r}")
~~~

sandbox 的相同 ID 比较不是完整 dict 合并：ID 相同直接保留 existing，不能用新同 ID 记录偷偷更新其他元信息；不同 ID 的受控生命周期替换须显式 Overwrite，而不是放松 reducer。{}、[] 和 None 在不同字段的意义不同，例如 artifacts=[] 不清空、viewed_images={} 清空、todos=[] 清空。task_notes 即使首次写入或 Overwrite 也经自定义 channel 规范化，避免依赖 reducer 仅在二次合并才触发。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:79–99](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L79-L99) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:103–112](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L103-L112) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:113–138](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L113-L138) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/task_continuity/state.py:99–104](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/task_continuity/state.py#L99-L104)


### M4. 沙箱生命周期与 Provider 抽象

#### D1 调用链追踪

SandboxMiddleware.before_agent/abefore_agent 先确定 user_id、thread_id、技能授权及当前恢复值；非 lazy 路径调用 provider.acquire/acquire_async，lazy 路径可在 abefore_model 启动预热。首个沙箱工具通过 wrap_tool_call/awrap_tool_call 与工具侧初始化路径会合。SandboxProvider 的 acquire(thread_id, *, user_id) 返回 ID，acquire_async 默认把同步实现移到线程；get/get_scoped 返回已跟踪客户端，release 表达调用者不再使用。抽象层还承载技能同步、网络策略事件及宿主关闭所有权，不能把 release 等同于 destroy。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py:247–331](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py#L247-L331) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py:367–444](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py#L367-L444) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/sandbox_provider.py:31–38](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/sandbox_provider.py#L31-L38) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/sandbox_provider.py:39–48](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/sandbox_provider.py#L39-L48) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/sandbox_provider.py:89–104](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/sandbox_provider.py#L89-L104)

LocalSandboxProvider.acquire 在构造线程路径映射后进入缓存锁，再次检查同一 (user_id,thread_id) 是否已有实例；命中复用，未命中创建 LocalSandbox 并按容量淘汰缓存。_setup_path_mappings 建公共映射，_build_thread_path_mappings 加入用户/线程数据和技能投影，LocalSandbox 的路径转换将虚拟路径解析到主机路径。这是路径命名与可见性约束，不是容器或操作系统隔离。bash_tool 入口默认拒绝主机 bash，必须显式启用 allow_host_bash；允许后进程在宿主上执行。LocalSandbox.execute_command 本身不重复该能力 gate，文件 API 的只读映射也不构成 shell 写保护。 [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/security.py:35–46](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/security.py#L35-L46) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/tools.py:2979–2989](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/tools.py#L2979-L2989)[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py:458–540](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py#L458-L540) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py:334–432](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py#L334-L432) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox.py:844–965](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox.py#L844-L965)

AIO 的调用和所有权分支如下，图仅描述实现的可能路径：

~~~text
acquire/acquire_async(user,thread)
 → AcquireSerializer 同一作用域串行化
 → 检查 shutdown、身份碰撞、隔离/隔离区策略
 ├─ active 命中且可复用 → 刷新使用/所有权 → 返回 ID
 ├─ warm 命中且健康 → warm 移出、active 注册 → 返回 ID
 └─ discovery/create
      → 跨进程文件锁（异步路径不阻塞事件循环等待锁）
      ├─ 已存在、策略兼容 → 注册 discovered
      ├─ 不兼容 → 获得销毁许可后替换，或拒绝/延迟
      └─ 不存在 → backend.create → 注册 created → 返回 ID
release → 活跃引用归零 → 关闭当前客户端 → warm 保留容器
        → 超时/LRU/显式 destroy/shutdown → 所有权检查 → backend.destroy
~~~

_create_backend 按配置选择本地容器 backend 或远端 provisioner；具体容器/HTTP 细节位于 SandboxBackend 实现。provider 负责身份、池和生命周期，backend 负责发现、创建、销毁及健康状态。AcquireSerializer 和文件锁保护创建，ownership lease 则裁定跨 Gateway 的销毁权，三者解决不同问题。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:349–384](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L349-L384) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:2319–2354](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L2319-L2354) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:2355–2398](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L2355-L2398) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:2399–2435](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L2399-L2435) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/backend.py:173–181](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/backend.py#L173-L181)

#### D2 状态流转

本模块读取 sandbox、thread_data、messages 及运行 context；可信 user/thread、lease owner、技能策略来自 runtime，不由历史 sandbox ID 授权。before_agent 的状态更新是部分映射；恢复值不符合当前作用域时，通过受控 Overwrite 切换 sandbox，普通 reducer 仍禁止两个非空不同 ID 合并。fork-restored 值可以来自父线程，因此 after_agent 特判，不能把父容器作为子线程资源归还。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py:247–331](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py#L247-L331) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py:462–493](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py#L462-L493) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:79–99](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L79-L99)

lazy 初始化有两种可见性：工具内部先改当前 runtime.state，随后 wrapper 比较调用前后的 sandbox ID，把变化补入 Command.update，让 LangGraph channel/checkpointer 能保存；仅修改 Python 字典并不等于持久状态提交。已有 Command 的 update 若属于不能安全合并的形态，不擅自改写。abefore_model 的预热 task 也不是 checkpoint 内容，恢复后需重新建立。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py:591–606](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py#L591-L606) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py:608–637](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py#L608-L637) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py:453–460](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py#L453-L460)

#### D3 并发与隔离

单 Agent 多工具并发和父子 Agent 共用容器时，sandbox/lease.py 通过 owner 和引用计数管理执行占用，避免一个 after_agent 释放仍在使用的容器。Local provider 用 threading.Lock 保护缓存，同 scope 双重检查；缓存键含 user_id/thread_id。子 Agent 是独立图上下文，但允许显式继承 sandbox 和 thread_data，因此文件副作用并未自动隔离，工具自己的并发规则仍然重要。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/lease.py:1–110](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/lease.py#L1-L110) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py:458–540](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py#L458-L540) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:1548–1716](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L1548-L1716)

跨 Gateway 的 SandboxOwnershipStore 提供 take、claim、renew、release、owner。MemoryOwnershipStore 只覆盖进程；RedisOwnershipStore 用 Lua 原子检查 token 和 TTL。take 可接管普通 own: 租约，却不能覆盖 del: 销毁标记；claim 只允许无主或自己，并在销毁时转 del:，防止已进入销毁的实例被当作普通可复用资源。renew 返回 RENEWED/LAPSED/LOST：已失效可重新建立租约，明确输给其他 owner 则丢弃本地跟踪而不停止对方容器。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/ownership/base.py:89–109](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/ownership/base.py#L89-L109) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/ownership/base.py:111–151](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/ownership/base.py#L111-L151) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/ownership/memory.py:83–92](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/ownership/memory.py#L83-L92) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/ownership/redis.py:1–124](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/ownership/redis.py#L1-L124)

续约同时覆盖 active 与 warm，并不依赖 idle_timeout。独立续约线程运行同步 store；本地 teardown reservation 与 acquire epoch 检查拦住迟到的 store 响应，避免“检查时属于我，返回时已重新获取”的竞态。该租约是销毁权协议，不代替工具授权，也不是所有文件写的事务锁。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:1441–1464](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L1441-L1464) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:482–554](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L482-L554) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:555–577](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L555-L577)

#### D4 生命周期

Middleware 的 before_agent/after_agent 对称的是使用权而非容器生死：获取或 retain，结束时 release。异步预热的清理可交给 worker 外层 fence 持有，直到整个执行收尾才释放；异常不能只依赖 after_agent 必然执行。lazy_init=True 表示按需建立、以后可凭作用域重新取得，跨轮复用依赖 provider 和容器是否仍存活，并不保证永远是同一 ID。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py:495–542](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/middleware.py#L495-L542) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/lease.py:640–829](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/lease.py#L640-L829)

Local release 不清缓存，reset/shutdown 才清除实例记录；它不会负责销毁主机文件。AIO release 进入 Warm Pool；过期扫描、LRU 淘汰和 shutdown 才可能 stop 容器。replicas 是池的软容量目标：优先淘汰最旧 warm，如果全是 active 可以超目标创建，不能作为严格并发配额。Warm Pool 用单调时钟计算空闲时长；被隔离的失败销毁还记录 generation，后续处理必须对应同一代资源。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py:578–588](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py#L578-L588) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py:603–608](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py#L603-L608) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:2807–2875](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L2807-L2875) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:2623–2683](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L2623-L2683) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/warm_pool_lifecycle.py:1–132](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/warm_pool_lifecycle.py#L1-L132)

#### D5 异常与边界

容易误读的边界有三处。第一，get 是通常面向已跟踪对象的热路径，但 Local get("local") 有兼容性的懒创建分支，不能宣称所有 get 永无 I/O。第二，Local release 注释关于“middleware 不调用”的旧描述不能覆盖实际 after_agent；真正调用链以上述实现为准。第三，ownership 不确定、策略不兼容、身份冲突或销毁中均可拒绝获取，属于避免错误复用/错误销毁的 fail-closed 路径。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py:555–577](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py#L555-L577) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py:578–588](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/sandbox/local/local_sandbox_provider.py#L578-L588) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:106–110](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L106-L110) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:131–140](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L131-L140)

release 的回收失败可能只记日志，不把已完成 run 改成失败；destroy 失败或“未能证明不存在”不能等价于删除成功，隔离记录承担后续约束。条件性风险是宿主硬退出、远端 provisioner 结果未知或 ownership 服务不可用时，清理可能延后；代码没有跨容器引擎与 Redis 的事务。这里是静态时序推断，不能据此断言已发生容器泄漏。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:2787–2806](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L2787-L2806) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:2495–2549](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L2495-L2549) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py:2575–2581](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/community/aio_sandbox/aio_sandbox_provider.py#L2575-L2581)

### M5. 记忆异步流水线

#### D1 调用链追踪

MemoryMiddleware.after_agent/aafter_agent 调用 _resolve_add_args：检查配置 enabled、thread_id 和 messages，捕获当前 user_id/agent_name、trace 与 locale，复制并按策略脱敏，再调用 manager.add/aadd。同步路径可能完成本地入队，异步路径仍 await 入队准入；立即返回的准确含义是“不等待后续提取模型和落盘”，不是整个 after_agent 没有任何等待。过滤 user/final AI 的实际规则位于默认 DeerMem 后端，不是 Middleware 本体。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/memory_middleware.py:196–230](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/memory_middleware.py#L196-L230) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/memory_middleware.py:236–254](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/memory_middleware.py#L236-L254) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/memory_middleware.py:256–271](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/memory_middleware.py#L256-L271) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/manager.py:1–180](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/manager.py#L1-L180)

DeerMem.model_post_init 创建配置、storage、LLM/updater 和队列；from_config 注入宿主路径与能力。add → filter_messages_for_memory → MemoryUpdateQueue.add_nowait/add → _enqueue_locked → Timer → _process_queue → MemoryUpdater.update_memory → _do_update_memory_sync_impl → LLM.invoke → _finalize_update → _apply_updates → FileMemoryStorage.apply_changes/_commit_changes_locked。队列线程用同步模型调用，避免在多轮 asyncio.run 之间重用绑定旧 event loop 的 HTTP 连接池。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deer_mem.py:116–158](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deer_mem.py#L116-L158) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deer_mem.py:211–248](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deer_mem.py#L211-L248) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/message_processing.py:157–220](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/message_processing.py#L157-L220) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py:271–366](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py#L271-L366) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py:1825–2050](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py#L1825-L2050)

队列的决策树保留了防抖、容量和特殊采样的区别：

~~~text
add(key=(thread,user,agent), 累积消息, signals)
 ├─ emergency snapshot → 独立快照、立即可处理、不推进普通 watermark
 ├─ key 已存在 → 以新累积历史替换，signals 取并集
 ├─ 新普通项且容量满、无重要 signal → 拒绝准入
 └─ 接受 → due=min(本次入队+debounce,首次入队+max_wait)
Timer 到期 → 锁内摘取 due 批次 → 锁外逐项提取/写入 → finally 重排 Timer
提取 → JSON/结构归一化 → 置信度及 scope/authority/durability 门控
     → 深拷贝当前记忆后应用变更 → revision 检查并提交
     ├─ revision 冲突 → reload，全量重新应用同一提取结果（最多三次）
     └─ 成功普通批次 → 推进 watermark
~~~

默认 debounce 为 30 秒、max_wait 为 300 秒，队列默认深度 1000； [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/config.py:142–159](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/config.py#L142-L159)触发时间不代表该时间内完成，已有批次、模型延迟和逐项 0.5 秒间隔会增加等待。紧急快照和带 signal 的准入规则意味着 max_depth 不是所有场景下的绝对内存上界。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py:1–108](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py#L1-L108) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py:170–239](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py#L170-L239) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py:1533–1634](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py#L1533-L1634)

#### D2 状态流转

输入来自 ThreadState.messages，但本模块不把 facts 写回 ThreadState；记忆是图状态外的数据源，下次装配/提示构造按当前身份读取。过滤保留用户内容和最终 AI 回复，排除 System、ToolMessage、AI 的工具调用中间步骤及无效/raw 工具表示，避免把临时工具输出、内部步骤和未完成计划提炼为长期事实。带结构标记的真实澄清回复可保留；仅上传文件的回合可以抑制其后 AI 提取，具体规则以 message_processing 为准，而不是简单 isinstance 两次判断。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/message_processing.py:157–220](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/message_processing.py#L157-L220) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/memory_middleware.py:196–230](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/memory_middleware.py#L196-L230)

默认 fact_confidence_threshold=0.7，等于阈值可以继续，但仍须通过 scope/durability/authority 等门控，不能认为“通过置信度即保存”。 [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/config.py:191–196](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/config.py#L191-L196)

下面10行显示阈值与 scope 检查的实际顺序，来源 [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py:2368–2377](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py#L2368-L2377)。

~~~python
        for fact_index, fact in enumerate(new_facts):
            confidence = fact.get("confidence", 0.5)
            if confidence >= config.fact_confidence_threshold:
                passed_threshold += 1
            rejection_reason = _fact_scope_gate_reason(fact)
            if rejection_reason is not None:
                reject_by_scope_gate("facts", rejection_reason)
                continue
            if confidence < config.fact_confidence_threshold:
                continue
~~~

updater 用 (thread,user,agent) watermark 确定未消费片段，同时有进程缓存和持久 sidecar；成功普通批次才前移，失败不前移，emergency 不推进。_finalize_update 先深拷贝再修改，避免提交失败污染共享缓存；存储支持 apply_changes 时计算 upsert/delete 和版本，带 expected_manifest_revision 提交；冲突 reload 后重新应用完整提取，不把基于旧快照的删除/容量决策拆成无条件点写。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py:1797–1824](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py#L1797-L1824) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py:1725–1743](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py#L1725-L1743) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py:1533–1634](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py#L1533-L1634)

实际观察：当前 canonical 布局不是预期的 {DEER_FLOW_HOME}/agents/{agent_name}/memory.json。宿主注入根目录后，用户摘要/manifest 位于 {root}/users/{safe_uid}/memory.json，事实位于 users/{uid}/agents/{lower_agent}/facts/{hashprefix}/{id}.md；默认 agent 使用 __default__ 桶。旧 agent memory.json 只出现在迁移兼容路径。standalone 还支持显式 storage_path、DEERMEM_DATA_DIR 或用户目录默认值，不能把宿主路径规则外推到独立包。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/paths.py:1–162](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/paths.py#L1-L162) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py:1143–1145](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py#L1143-L1145)

#### D3 并发与隔离

queue 用 threading.Lock 维护待处理表、processing 和 Timer，锁内出队、锁外调用 LLM，单消费者顺序提取；同键替换累积历史避免每次对同一整段历史重复排队。不同子 Agent 可共享同一 manager/队列，但键含 user 和 agent；facts 按用户/agent 隔离，用户摘要在该用户范围内共享，这不是完全每 agent 独立的所有记忆。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py:52–60](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py#L52-L60) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py:271–366](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py#L271-L366) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/paths.py:1–162](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/paths.py#L1-L162)

多 Gateway 不共享 Python 队列，但 FileMemoryStorage 有 scope RLock、进程文件锁、manifest/fact revision 检查；锁覆盖读—计算—写对应的文件操作，revision 还保护此前锁外 LLM 提取基于的快照。检索索引通知有自己的兼容性与失效处理，不等于文件提交和远端索引原子提交。保护要求实例访问同一实际文件系统及有效文件锁；部署存储语义未确认。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py:494–547](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py#L494-L547) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py:985–988](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py#L985-L988) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py:1878–1977](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py#L1878-L1977) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py:1008–1086](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py#L1008-L1086)

#### D4 生命周期

后台资源包括 daemon Timer、队列项、LLM/检索客户端、缓存、sidecar 和事实文件。_process_queue 在 finally 清 processing 并重新调度；clear/cancel_by_agent 可删除待处理项，却不能据此证明已开始提取立即停止。flush/flush_sync 处理待消费工作，flush_sync 还考虑当前 worker；宿主关闭尽力调用清理。进程硬退出可能丢失尚在内存中的队列，持久 checkpoint 不能替队列提供可靠消息投递。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py:390–459](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py#L390-L459) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py:470–514](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py#L470-L514) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deer_mem.py:591–595](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deer_mem.py#L591-L595)

_atomic_write 使用临时文件、flush/fsync、replace 和父目录同步；涉及 manifest 与多个 Markdown fact 的提交另有 journal/backup 与 _recover_if_needed。不能把“单文件原子替换”说成“多个文件的单次原子事务”。revision 与恢复日志维持库内一致性，成功返回前后的文件操作和索引通知仍有各自故障窗口。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py:476–492](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py#L476-L492) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py:1322–1474](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py#L1322-L1474) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py:1274–1321](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py#L1274-L1321)

#### D5 异常与边界

源码边界：DeerMem.add 只把 QueueFull 记录并跳过，下一轮可能重新馈入尚未推进水位的历史；MemoryMiddleware 本身没有通用 catch，manager 初始化、脱敏或其他后端异常仍可能沿 after_agent 传播。MemoryManager.aadd 默认直接调用同步 add，async 接口并不自动把任意插件后端的阻塞工作移到线程。 [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deer_mem.py:211–248](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deer_mem.py#L211-L248) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/manager.py:574–584](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/manager.py#L574-L584)

另一个已确认的注释/实现矛盾在同步 MemoryUpdater.update_memory：当当前线程已有活动 event loop，它提交 _SYNC_MEMORY_UPDATER_EXECUTOR 后立即 future.result()，调用线程仍同步等待；注释所称“不阻塞 caller loop”不能成立。触发前提是异步代码直接调用这一同步接口，普通 Timer 消费路径不经过该分支；真正 aupdate_memory 使用 await asyncio.to_thread。此结论来自等待语句，不依赖延迟实测。 [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py:2123–2146](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py#L2123-L2146) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py:1635–1666](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py#L1635-L1666)

模型 JSON 无效、置信度不合规、scope/authority 不满足、容量淘汰、版本竞争和文件写失败是不同分支。门控会丢弃候选事实；解析/存储失败不应被读成事实已保存。用户记忆提交后，关联项目记忆阶段还可能失败；实现会避免简单重放已消费用户写入，因而“整个 batch 全成功或全失败”也不成立。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py:448–468](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py#L448-L468) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py:2157–2717](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py#L2157-L2717) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py:1825–2050](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py#L1825-L2050)

“主流程不阻塞”和“记忆绝不丢失”不能互相推出：after_agent 只保证提交尝试与错误边界，队列不持久化，关闭 flush 也是尽力而为。已 drain 表示队列消费完，不保证每项 LLM/写入都成功。提取模型输出是待校验输入，置信度是过滤参数而非事实真伪证明；手工事实、删除与合并另有来源约束，不能将模型任意文本直接当文件路径。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py:271–366](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/queue.py#L271-L366) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py:268–447](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/updater.py#L268-L447) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py:375–388](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/memory/backends/deermem/deermem/core/storage.py#L375-L388)


### M6. 子智能体委派限流

#### D1 调用链追踪

模型生成 task 调用后，SubagentLimitMiddleware.after_model/aafter_model 调用 _truncate_task_calls，读取最后一条 AIMessage 与当前 run 的 delegations，先于 ToolNode 执行删去超额调用。保留下来的 task_tool 才会解析目标 agent、创建 SubagentExecutor 并执行；_aexecute 再向 SubagentExecutionCapacity.slot 申请实际运行槽位，获准后进入 _aexecute_admitted，后者组装子图并消费 agent.astream。软提示、每次响应截断、全 run 委派预算和进程执行容量是四个层次。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/subagent_limit_middleware.py:133–183](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/subagent_limit_middleware.py#L133-L183) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/tools/builtins/task_tool.py:819–1472](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/tools/builtins/task_tool.py#L819-L1472) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:1717–1757](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L1717-L1757) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/capacity.py:143–150](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/capacity.py#L143-L150)

配额计算的实际三行如下，来源 [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/subagent_limit_middleware.py:154–156](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/subagent_limit_middleware.py#L154-L156)。

~~~python
        prior_delegation_count = _count_prior_delegations(state.get("delegations"), run_id=run_id)
        remaining_total = max(0, self.max_total - prior_delegation_count)
        allowed_task_calls = min(self.max_concurrent, remaining_total)
~~~

max_concurrent 限制“本条模型响应内可以保留多少个 task”，不直接统计所有线程正在运行的任务；max_total 限制当前 run 已登记的委派总数。代码计算 allowed=min(max_concurrent,max(0,max_total-prior))，只删除多余 task，其他工具原顺序保留。默认构造为 3/6，分别 clamp 到 [1,64]/[1,50]，调用方还会按执行容量缩小。Orchestration 提示向模型说明何时委派、预算与汇总职责；模型不遵守提示时，中间件仍执行程序限制。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/subagent_limit_middleware.py:104–125](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/subagent_limit_middleware.py#L104-L125) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/config/subagents_config.py:1–120](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/config/subagents_config.py#L1-L120) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/prompt.py:451–662](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/prompt.py#L451-L662)

~~~text
after_model
 ├─ 无 messages / 最后非 AI / 无 task → 不更新
 └─ task 数 > allowed
      → 移除超额 task、清理消息的所有工具调用表示
      ├─ remaining_total > 0 → 仅截断
      └─ remaining_total == 0 → 加提示、stop_reason=subagent_limit_capped
剩余 task → executor
 ├─ capacity 不足且禁止排队 → FAILED(admission_failure)
 └─ 等待/取得 slot → RUNNING → 子图循环
       ├─ 正常/可用的预算内部分结果 → COMPLETED（可能带 stop_reason）
       ├─ 工具直接返回错误/模型失败 → FAILED
       └─ 取消/超时 → 对应子任务终态并收尾
~~~

[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:1758–2246](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L1758-L2246) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:2317–2501](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L2317-L2501)

#### D2 状态流转

中间件读取 messages、delegations，只返回部分 messages 更新。clone_ai_message_with_tool_calls 保留消息 ID，使 reducer 替换原 AIMessage，同时清除 raw/additional_kwargs/content 中已删调用，不能只改 tool_calls 属性而留下另一份可被后续解析的副本。已移除调用从未执行，不生成伪 ToolMessage；保留调用才需要一一关联的工具反馈。DanglingToolCallMiddleware 处理历史或中断留下的未配对调用，不能用它替代本次消息的一致性改写。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_call_metadata.py:87–130](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_call_metadata.py#L87-L130) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/dangling_tool_call_middleware.py:1–160](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/dangling_tool_call_middleware.py#L1-L160)

prior 以 run_id 过滤并对 (tool_call_id,dispatch_message_id) 去重；provider 在后续 AI 响应复用同一 call ID 时仍算不同委派。durable ledger 保证同一 run 多次规划不会重置预算。子任务状态保存在 SubagentResult，再通过 task 工具的结果和 delegation 更新回到父图；子图只显式继承 sandbox/thread_data/uploaded_files 等，不把整个父 messages 直接当自己历史，任务快照也有明确消息形式。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/subagent_limit_middleware.py:91–103](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/subagent_limit_middleware.py#L91-L103) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py:258–298](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/thread_state.py#L258-L298) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:1548–1716](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L1548-L1716)

#### D3 并发与隔离

SubagentLimitMiddleware 本身不持有可变全局计数，依赖图当前 ledger，因而不是 Redis 配额。相同 thread 的 run 并发准入由 RunManager 和持久 execution fence 负责；直接 SDK 绕开该层时不能从中间件推导多实例全局限额。SubagentExecutionCapacity 使用 asyncio.Lock 保护当前 event loop 的 running/waiter 状态，以 Future 排队；controller 活跃时拒绝跨 loop 迁移，空闲才允许重建，并对取消后的移除与释放单独处理；slot 的 finally 覆盖正常与异常。该容量是宿主进程/显式 runtime 内资源，不是全体 Gateway 共享的分布式 semaphore。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/capacity.py:62–86](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/capacity.py#L62-L86) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/capacity.py:97–108](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/capacity.py#L97-L108) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/capacity.py:118–141](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/capacity.py#L118-L141) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py:2413–2704](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/manager.py#L2413-L2704)

SubagentResult 用 _state_lock 保证终态不会被迟到成功覆盖，另用事件表达 cancel 与 execution_done。同步/后台任务可在隔离 event loop 线程执行；隔离的是调度上下文，不是内存、进程、权限或文件。子图创建不复用父 checkpointer，运行 context 显式传 user_role、授权属性、approval runtime、sandbox lease owner 和命令 scope；共用 sandbox 仍可能读写同一文件。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:267–310](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L267-L310) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:857–894](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L857-L894) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:1131–1326](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L1131-L1326) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:1758–2246](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L1758-L2246)

#### D4 生命周期

任务建立 result、future、输入门、token collector、子图和独立 sandbox 使用权；execute_async 注册后台结果，supervisor 维护实际执行结束，cleanup 不能仅按“结果已终态”就删除仍运行的对象。_aexecute finally 关闭 input_gate、置 execution_done_event；_aexecute_admitted 的 finally 关闭 agent stream、排空 sandbox 预热清理、释放执行 lease。SubagentRuntime.start/stop 管理宿主工作和队列；模块级隔离 loop 有独立关闭逻辑。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:1717–1757](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L1717-L1757) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:1758–2246](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L1758-L2246) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:2591–2629](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L2591-L2629) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:682–733](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L682-L733) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/runtime.py:131–168](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/runtime.py#L131-L168)

#### D5 异常与边界

没有 runtime.run_id 时不是重置为零：实现记录警告，保守统计线程全部 delegations，可能较早达到总预算。预算耗尽仅阻止新委派，不能撤销已启动任务。GraphRecursionError 可把最后可用 AI 文本作为部分完成，并带 turn_capped；所以 completed 不必然等于满足父任务验收。直接返回工具 status=error 则转 FAILED，不能把“图正常结束”当作“业务成功”。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/subagent_limit_middleware.py:133–183](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/subagent_limit_middleware.py#L133-L183) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:1758–2246](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L1758-L2246)

子 Agent 取消区分请求、stream 关闭、沙箱进程取消和实际执行结束；超时等待者不能证明底层立即停止。任务 terminal 状态和 execution_done_event 分开正是为了避免过早释放/删除。条件性风险是共享文件副作用、宿主退出和多实例独立容量产生的总资源放大；已有代码不能支持“全系统严格最多三个子任务”的结论。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:2317–2501](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L2317-L2501) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:2630–2648](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L2630-L2648)

### M7. 循环检测

#### D1 调用链追踪

装配器按配置创建 LoopDetectionMiddleware；after_model/aafter_model → _apply → _track_and_check 对本轮 AI 工具调用做两层检测。低阈值返回 warning 并排入 pending；下一次 wrap_model_call/awrap_model_call → _drain_pending_warnings → _inject_warnings，在当前模型请求中追加 HumanMessage。高阈值移除所有待执行工具调用、追加停止说明并标 stop_reason，阻止本批工具进入执行。工具调用元数据的一致清理由 clone_ai_message_with_tool_calls 负责。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py:485–822](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/lead_agent/agent.py#L485-L822) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:818–869](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L818-L869) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:579–765](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L579-L765) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_call_metadata.py:87–130](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/tool_call_metadata.py#L87-L130)

_hash_tool_calls 将每个调用的规范化 name/args 稳定序列化，排序后把这一轮调用多重集合生成 MD5 前 12 位。排序消除并行调用排列差异，重复元素仍保留；MD5 在这里是非安全去重标记，不提供防碰撞证明。read_file 的行号/范围有归一化；已知工具的 UI description 不影响重复判定，write_file 的真实内容则参与；未知工具保留其参数，避免过度归一化吞掉业务差异。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:191–224](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L191-L224) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:162–190](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L162-L190) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:225–254](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L225-L254)

~~~text
after_model 最后一条 AI 有 tool_calls?
 ├─ 否 → 无处理
 └─ 是 → 同 (thread,run) 的 hash 滑动窗口 + 工具类型频率窗口
       ├─ 任一 hard 阈值达到 → 删除本轮全部 calls、loop_capped
       ├─ 新 warning 阈值达到 → 本批仍执行，下一轮请求注入 warning
       └─ 未达到 → 照常执行
wrap_model_call
 → 取出 pending → 请求内注入 → 调模型
   └─ 异常 → 恢复已取出的 warning，再向上抛
after_agent → 清本 run pending 与 fallback identity
~~~

默认 hash window=20、warn=3、hard=5；工具类型默认 warn=30/hard=50，允许按工具覆盖，频率窗口会扩到足以达到最大阈值。hash 候选 warning 不会提前 return 跳过频率硬限制，后者可覆盖前者。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:328–405](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L328-L405) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:579–765](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L579-L765)

#### D2 状态流转

图状态读取 messages，hard stop 返回同 ID AIMessage 的部分替换；warning 只改变下一次 ModelRequest.messages，不追加到 ThreadState 历史。这避免在 AI tool_calls 和 ToolMessage 之间插入未配对 HumanMessage。pending_warnings 按 (thread_id,run_id) 隔离，history、warned hash、工具 deque/Counter 都是中间件实例内状态，不是 reducer/checkpoint 字段。恢复图不等于恢复此前所有检测计数。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:940–957](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L940-L957) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:567–578](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L567-L578) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:818–869](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L818-L869)

实际观察：before_agent/abefore_agent 当前是 no-op；预期背景所说的“启动时清 stale warning”不成立，清理由 after_agent 和有界淘汰负责。after_agent 清当前 pending/fallback 身份，但不会清空所有历史；reset 才整体重置。run_id 键存在即权威，包括显式 None；只有缺键才依次尝试 runtime.execution_info.run_id，再以 runtime.control（没有则 runtime）作 anchor 映射随机 token，强引用避免对象地址重用。thread_id 缺失则使用 "default"。因此复用同一实例且反复显式传 None 的嵌入路径可能共用 (thread,None) 历史；标准 Gateway 注入的真实 thread/run 标识才提供明确范围。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:883–888](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L883-L888) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:437–478](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L437-L478) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:903–907](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L903-L907) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:984–1017](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L984-L1017)

#### D3 并发与隔离

thread_id 来自 runtime.context，run_id 进一步区分同线程运行；共享同一 middleware 的线程访问以 threading.Lock 保护 history、pending、LRU 和频率计数。锁只覆盖短内存操作，不跨模型网络调用。每个子图可有自己的 middleware 实例，审计 recorder 由 context 注入并携带 agent 标识；不因此共享同一父子总循环预算。多 Gateway 各自维护内存窗口，不是 Redis 计数器；运行 lease 防止旧 worker 写终态，不会把循环历史自动复制到接管实例。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:430–436](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L430-L436) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:513–516](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L513-L516) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:579–765](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L579-L765) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py:1758–2246](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/subagents/executor.py#L1758-L2246)

#### D4 生命周期

资源是有上限的内存历史、Counter/deque、pending warnings 和后备 run identity。_evict_if_needed 以 LRU 淘汰历史，_prune_pending_warning_state_locked 限制 warning 状态；after_agent 释放当前 pending，reset 清所有。频率计算对每个调用更新 Counter 为 O(1)，窗口驱逐摊销 O(1)；hash 排序对本批 k 次调用为 O(k log k)，序列化成本随参数大小增长。这里是代码复杂度，未声称任何实测性能。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:521–537](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L521-L537) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:554–566](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L554-L566) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:225–254](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L225-L254)

#### D5 异常与边界

低阈值不会阻止当前工具执行，高阈值则直接终止本轮 calls；两者结合，不是只写一条日志或永远靠模型自觉。hard stop 不抛专用异常，也不保证再调模型生成“自然结束语”，它写停止说明并由图的后续条件结束；stop_reason 使 worker/子任务结果能表示受限完成。模型异常后恢复 pending，避免 warning 因“已取出但请求失败”永久丢失。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:818–869](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L818-L869) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:959–970](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L959-L970) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:972–983](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L972-L983)

边界是工具“频率高”不证明任务无进展：合法遍历大量不同文件也会达到类型阈值；按工具覆盖和滑动衰减是现有可调机制。工具参数表示差异也可能影响 hash，规范化只覆盖明确工具规则。进程重启/新图实例丢失计数是内存设计的适用边界，不能宣称 checkpoint 提供累计循环防护。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:124–149](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L124-L149) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py:579–765](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/agents/middlewares/loop_detection_middleware.py#L579-L765)


### M8. 流式输出双路径

#### D1 调用链追踪

Gateway 入口 stream_run/create_run 调用 start_run 完成准入并启动后台 worker；run_agent._stream_once 消费 agent.astream，多 mode 的每项先经过 _unpack_stream_item 解析 (namespace,mode,chunk)，_compose_sse_event 把子图命名空间编码为 mode|ns1|ns2，serialize 转换可传输数据，再调用 StreamBridge.publish(run_id,event,data)。HTTP 一侧 sse_consumer 订阅 bridge，format_sse 组装 event/id/data 行。producer 与 HTTP 消费者分离，创建返回、图执行和网络发送不是同一时刻。[backend/app/gateway/routers/thread_runs.py:1091–1138](../backend/app/gateway/routers/thread_runs.py#L1091-L1138) [backend/app/gateway/services.py:2295–3064](../backend/app/gateway/services.py#L2295-L3064) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:1691–1816](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L1691-L1816) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:3945–3977](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L3945-L3977) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:3978–3992](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L3978-L3992) [backend/app/gateway/services.py:3261–3383](../backend/app/gateway/services.py#L3261-L3383)

实际观察：MemoryStreamBridge 当前是 events:list + asyncio.Condition 的有界日志，预期“asyncio Queue”及 client 注释中的 queue 描述已经过时。publish 深拷贝数据、追加事件、裁剪旧项、notify_all；每个 subscribe 有独立绝对 offset，不是多个消费者争抢一个 Queue 元素。RedisStreamBridge 则用 Redis Streams 的 XADD/XREAD，在多 Gateway 间共享事件和 producer lease。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/stream_bridge/memory.py:119–131](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/stream_bridge/memory.py#L119-L131) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/stream_bridge/memory.py:138–200](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/stream_bridge/memory.py#L138-L200) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/stream_bridge/redis.py:741–762](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/stream_bridge/redis.py#L741-L762) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/stream_bridge/redis.py:834–1011](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/stream_bridge/redis.py#L834-L1011)

DeerFlowClient.stream → _stream_turn → _ensure_agent → 同进程 agent.stream，直接 yield Python StreamEvent；chat 消费这个迭代器拼答案。当前公开路径是同步生成器，没有证据支持把它描述为已有 client.astream 异步 API。它复用图装配与状态机制，但不走 Gateway RunManager/SSE。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/client.py:859–912](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/client.py#L859-L912) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/client.py:913–1313](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/client.py#L913-L1313) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/client.py:1314–1341](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/client.py#L1314-L1341)

~~~text
Gateway：
后台 agent.astream → 序列化 → bridge 有界保留
                                  ├─ creator SSE
                                  ├─ join SSE（独立游标）
                                  └─ 重连 Last-Event-ID → replay 或 gap
SDK：
调用方 next() → agent.stream → Python event → 调用方处理 → 再 next()
~~~

#### D2 状态流转

流桥不写 ThreadState；values/updates/messages 是图状态或增量的观察结果，不能把“token 已看见”当作已提交最终 channel。worker 还会在发送前区分 root/subgraph，防止子图 values 被当作根线程状态。_MessageSeqStamper 把已持久消息的 feed seq 附到输出副本，不回写 checkpoint；查不到的 seq 会随 journal generation 前进再查询，命中后缓存。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:1076–1084](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L1076-L1084) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py:3978–3992](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/runs/worker.py#L3978-L3992)

Last-Event-ID 只控制事件游标，不回滚或重启图。memory ID 是时间戳-序号，确认在保留范围内的 ID 从其后回放；落后保留水位返回 StreamGap；恰好等于最后淘汰事件可从当前最早项续接；其他未知 ID 有保守/兼容分支。Redis 对合法未来游标、已观察 stream 消失和保留区间丢失显式给 gap。sse_consumer 发送 recovery=reload_durable_state 的 gap 后返回，**它本身不替客户端加载状态**。[backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/stream_bridge/memory.py:82–112](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/stream_bridge/memory.py#L82-L112) [backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/stream_bridge/redis.py:834–1011](../backend/packages/harness/deerflow/runtime/stream_bridge/redis.py#L834-L1011) [backend/app/gateway/services.py:3261–3383](../backend/app/gateway/services.py#L3261-L3383)

#### D3 并发与隔离
